use_groups: Boolean - use group(table_code) syntax for subject/profile tables (optional, auto-detected)\n    - variables: Override default variables with custom list (optional)\n\n    Examples:\n    - {"year": 2023, "dataset": "acs/acs5", "table_code": "B01003", "geo_for": "county:*", "geo_in": "state:06"}\n    - {"year": 2023, "dataset": "acs/acs5/subject", "table_code": "S0101", "geo_for": "state:*", "use_groups": true}\n    - \n    Execute a Census API query and fetch actual data.\n    \n    Supports complex Census API patterns including:\n    - Multiple dataset categories (acs/acs5, acs/acs5/subject, acs/acs1/profile, etc.)\n    - Complex geography hierarchies (CBSAs, Metropolitan Divisions, NECTAs)\n    - Group syntax for subject tables (group(S0101))\n    - Multi-level geography constraints with chained in= clauses\n    \n    Input must be valid JSON with these fields:\n    - year: Census year (required)\n    - dataset: Dataset path like "acs/acs5", "acs/acs5/subject", "acs/acs1/profile" (required)\n    - variables: List of variable codes OR "group(TABLE_CODE)" for subject tables (required)\n    - geo_for: Geography for clause dict like {"county": "*", "state (or part)": "*"} (required)\n    - geo_in: Geography in clause dict(s) - can be complex like {"state": "06", "metropolitan statistical area/micropolitan statistical area": "35620"} (optional)\n    - geo_in_chained: For complex nested queries, array of geo_in objects to chain together (optional)\n    \n    Examples:\n    - Simple: {"year": 2023, "dataset": "acs/acs5", "variables": ["NAME", "B01003_001E"], "geo_for": {"county": "*"}, "geo_in": {"state": "06"}}\n    - Subject table: {"year": 2023, "dataset": "acs/acs5/subject", "variables": ["group(S0101)"], "geo_for": {"state": "*"}}\n    - Complex CBSA: {"year": 2023, "dataset": "acs/acs5", "variables": ["NAME", "B01001_001E"], "geo_for": {"state (or part)": "*"}, "geo_in": {"metropolitan statistical area/micropolitan statistical area": "35620", "metropolitan division": "35614"}}\n    \ncreate_table - \n    Export census data as formatted tables\n    \n    Input must be valid JSON with these fields:\n    - format: Output format (csv, excel, html) - defaults to csv\n    - filename: Optional custom filename (without extension)\n    - title: Table title/description (optional)\n    - data: Census data dict from census_api_call tool\n    \nbuild_census_url - \n    Build a Census API URL from components with support for complex Census API patterns.\n    \n    Supports all dataset categories and complex geography hierarchies from CENSUS_DISCUSSION.md:\n    - Detail tables (B01003), Subject tables (S0101), Profile tables (DP03)\n    - Complex geography hierarchies (CBSAs, Metropolitan Divisions, NECTAs)\n    - Group syntax for subject tables and multi-level geo constraints\n\n    Input must be valid JSON:\n    - year: Census year (required)\n    - dataset: Dataset like "acs/acs5", "acs/acs5/subject", "acs/acs1/profile" (required)\n    - table_code: Table code like "B01003", "S0101", "DP03" (required)\n    - table_category: "detail", "subject", "profile", "cprofile", "spp" (optional)\n    - geo_for: Geography for clause like "county:*" or complex {"county": "*", "state (or part)": "*"} (required)\n    - geo_in: Geography in clause like "state:06" or complex multi-level dict (optional)\n    - use_groups: Boolean - use group(table_code) syntax for subject/profile tables (optional, auto-detected)\n    - variables: Override default variables with custom list (optional)\n\n    Examples:\n    - {"year": 2023, "dataset": "acs/acs5", "table_code": "B01003", "geo_for": "county:*", "geo_in": "state:06"}\n    - {"year": 2023, "dataset": "acs/acs5/subject", "table_code": "S0101", "geo_for": "state:*", "use_groups": true}\n    - {"year": 2023, "dataset": "acs/acs5", "table_code": "B01001", "geo_for": {"state (or part)": "*"}, "geo_in": {"metropolitan statistical area/micropolitan statistical area": "35620"}}\n    \nresolve_area_name - \n    Resolve a friendly geography name to its Census code.\n    \n    Use this when you need to convert a single area name to its FIPS code.\n    \n    Input must be valid JSON with these fields:\n    - name: Area name (required)\n    - geography_type: Geography level (default: "state")\n    - dataset: Census dataset (default: "acs/acs5")\n    - year: Year (default: 2023)\n    - parent: Parent geography dict (optional)\n    \n    Examples:\n    - {"name": "California", "geography_type": "state"}\n    - {"name": "Los Angeles County", "geography_type": "county", "parent": {"state": "06"}}\n    \ncreate_chart - \n    Create data visualizations from census data\n    \n    Supports both single-series and multi-series charts (auto-detected).\n    Multi-series charts automatically group by geography when multiple areas are present.\n    \n    Input must be valid JSON with these fields:\n    -
